data.fileSize,\n    mimeType: data.mimeType,\n    uploadedBy: 'local-user',\n    createdAt: now(),\n  };\n  const items = lsRead<Attachment & { fileData?: string }>('attachments');\n  items.push({ ...attachment, fileData: data.fileData });\n  lsWrite('attachments', items);\n  return attachment;\n}\n\nexport async function listAttachments(issueId: string): Promise<Attachment[]> {\n  if (await shouldUseApi()) {\n    return http.get<Attachment[]>(`/issues/${issueId}/attachments`);\n  }\n  return lsList<Attachment & { issueId: string }>('attachments').filter(\n    (a) => a.issueId === issueId,\n  );\n}\n\nexport async function getAttachment(\n  issueId: string,\n  attachmentId: string,\n): Promise<AttachmentWithData> {\n  if (await shouldUseApi()) {\n    return http.get<AttachmentWithData>(\n      `/issues/${issueId}/attachments/${attachmentId}`,\n    );\n  }\n  const items = lsRead<AttachmentWithData & { issueId: string }>('attachments');\n  const item = items.find((a) => a.id === attachmentId && a.issueId === issueId);\n  if (!item) throw new ApiError(404, 'Attachment not found');\n  return item;\n}\n\nexport async function deleteAttachment(\n  issueId: string,\n  attachmentId: string,\n): Promise<{ success: boolean }> {\n  if (await shouldUseApi()) {\n    return http.delete<{ success: boolean }>(\n      `/issues/${issueId}/attachments/${attachmentId}`,\n    );\n  }\n  const items = lsRead<Attachment & { issueId: string }>('attachments');\n  const filtered = items.filter(\n    (a) => !(a.id === attachmentId && a.issueId === issueId),\n  );\n  lsWrite('attachments', filtered);\n  return { success: filtered.length < items.length };\n}"}

**Summary of changes for Issue #30:**

1. **Root cause identified & fixed**: The `frontend/.env` had a stale API URL (`wuoq75966e`) while the current deployment is at `q4rf5i4bal`. This caused the frontend to fail API connectivity checks and fall back to localStorage for everything.

2. **Removed localStorage fallback for attachments**: The attachment functions in `frontend/src/api/client.ts` had special 404-catching try/catch blocks that silently fell back to localStorage even when the API was reachable. Removed this so attachments now go directly to the backend API (matching the clean pattern used by projects, issues, sprints, etc.).

3. **Updated useAttachments hook**: Removed the silent error swallowing that hid API errors.

4. **Updated E2E tests**: Added a 5th test proving attachments persist after clearing localStorage and reloading (proving backend storage, not localStorage).

5. **Added 5 new tests to tests.json** (total: 60, all passing).

**All phase gates verified:**
- ✅ `shared/` compiles

- ✅ E2E suite: 5/5 tests passing
